Separately, answer-revealing turns are followed by less independent student work on every base, a result that is judge-invariant by construction. In this controlled setting, general-purpose helpfulness is not a reliable pedagogy signal. Tutor evaluation should pair pedagogy-targeted rubrics with deterministic process measures.

</details>


### [35] [Can Agents Deceive? Evaluating Reasoning and Deception in ParliamentBench using a Social Deduction Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28146)
*Niklas Bauer,Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Akiko Aizawa,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp,Terry Ruas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出基于 Secret Hitler 游戏的开源基准 ParliamentBench，评估 16 个大语言模型在欺骗、说服和信息不对称推理场景下的表现，引入三项新指标量化社交推理、推理与欺骗一致性。实验显示顶尖模型（GPT-5.4、Kimi K2.5、Grok 4.1 Fast、DeepSeek 3.1 Terminus）在合作与欺骗角色均表现强劲，但多数模型难以维持全程一致的欺骗人设，欺骗保持率低于 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在医疗、法律等高风险场景部署为智能体，理解其欺骗能力成为安全研究的核心。受控的社交推理游戏提供了可复现的代理环境，能隔离并评估复杂的对抗行为。

Method: 构建基于 Secret Hitler 的开源框架 ParliamentBench，让 16 个 LLM 进行 1,600 场模拟对弈（模型间、模型对人类、与大量在线人类对局对比），并引入三个新指标分别度量社交推理能力、推理能力和欺骗一致性。

Result: 前沿模型在合作与欺骗角色均达强性能，形成显著的第一梯队（GPT-5.4、Kimi K2.5、Grok 4.1 Fast、DeepSeek 3.1 Terminus）；最弱模型低于随机基线（33%）和简单算法基线（45%）；绝大多数模型无法维持全局一致的欺骗人设，欺骗保持率跌破 50%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在欺骗能力上存在显著分层：顶尖模型已具备高水平的欺骗与推理综合能力，但主流模型在长程欺骗一致性上仍有明显短板，未来需重点提升欺骗行为的稳定性与可控性，以保障高风险应用的安全性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as agents in high-stakes settings, such as medical and legal systems, understanding their deceptive capabilities is fundamental to safety. Controlled social deduction games provide a reproducible proxy for isolating and evaluating these complex adversarial behaviors. We present the open-source benchmark framework ParliamentBench based on the game Secret Hitler to evaluate LLMs in scenarios that require deception, persuasion, and reasoning under information asymmetry. We evaluate 16 LLMs across 1,600 simulated matches playing each other, playing against humans, and compare them against a large set of online games. We introduce three novel metrics that isolate social deduction, reasoning, and deceptive consistency. Our experiments reveal that frontier models achieve strong performance across cooperative and deceptive roles, with a strong top-four cluster (GPT-5.4, Kimi K2.5, Grok 4.1 Fast, and DeepSeek 3.1 Terminus), whereas the weakest models fall short of random (33%) and simple algorithmic (45%) baselines. Most LLMs struggle to maintain a consistent deceptive persona throughout an entire game, with deception retention dropping below 50%.

</details>


### [36] [RRM: Experience-Driven Reflective Retrieval Memory for Long-Horizon Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28156)
*Jingxiang Fan,Junbao Zhuo,Bochao Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了反思检索记忆（RRM）框架，通过反思经验记忆提升长视频多模态推理的检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态长时记忆代理侧重于存储内容而非检索策略，缺乏从失败轨迹中诊断并调整检索策略的机制。

Method: RRM 在实体中心的多模态记忆图中引入反思经验记忆，从历史任务轨迹中提炼可迁移的程序性检索知识，将检索经验转化为查询级指导，同时答案生成仍依赖当前视频的事实证据；并通过生命周期管理（使用频率、复用反馈、时间衰减）调节经验记忆。

Result: 在 M3-Bench-Robot、M3-Bench-Web 和 Video-MME-Long 基准上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 反思检索记忆对长时程多模态推理有效。

Abstract: Existing multimodal long-term memory agents use external memory to overcome the limited context available for long videos. However, most methods emphasize what to store rather than how stored memory should be retrieved. When retrieval becomes inaccurate or repeatedly fails to obtain useful evidence, existing agents lack mechanisms to diagnose failures from previous task trajectories and adapt future search strategies.We introduce Reflective Retrieval Memory (RRM), a reflective memory framework for long-horizon multimodal reasoning. RRM augments an entity-centric multimodal memory graph with reflective experience memory, which distills transferable procedural retrieval knowledge from historical task trajectories. Unlike episodic and semantic memories that preserve factual evidence from the current video, reflective experience memory captures reusable search strategies across tasks. RRM converts retrieved experiences into query-level guidance, while answer generation remains conditioned only on factual evidence newly retrieved from the current video. A lifecycle management mechanism further regulates experience memory through usage frequency, reuse feedback, and temporal decay, thereby reducing redundancy and noise. RRM consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches on M3-Bench-Robot, M3-Bench-Web, and Video-MME-Long, demonstrating the effectiveness of reflective retrieval memory for long-horizon multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [37] [Where and When to Commit: Candidate-Aware Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28166)
*Chia-Ming Lee,Ming-Ching Chang,Xin Li,Yu-Lun Liu,Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LATCH（Localized Acceleration with Tracked-Candidate Halting），一种无需训练的早退框架，用于加速扩散语言模型（DLM）的生成。LATCH 将“停止时机”与“加速位置”解耦：Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC) 基于动态提取的候选片段的置信度与 argmax 稳定性决定全局终止；Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC) 对非最终块应用廉价局部规则加速，最终块仍由 CVC 管控。实验在 11 个零样本任务上（LLaDA 与 Dream）显示，LATCH 在保持全解码精度 2 个百分点内的同时，实现短答案任务 9.3-17.8×、长推理任务 2.0-3.3× 的端到端吞吐加速，且单一超参数集跨骨干网络零调迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DLM 早退方法依赖固定区域置信度统计或调度相关规则，证据粗糙，导致在长链式思维输出中过早终止（答案仅在末尾稳定）。自适应采样虽能控制位置提交速度，但不验证输出是否已稳定。因此需要一种训练免、候选感知的框架，将终止决策与加速决策分离，并各自匹配其作用域的证据。

Method: LATCH 包含两个正交组件：(1) Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC)：使用任务指定的输出格式解析器动态提取候选片段，仅当该片段的置信度与持续 argmax 稳定性均满足阈值时才允许全局终止。(2) Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC)：将序列划分为块，对非最终块应用廉价局部提交规则加速，最终块及全局终止仍由 CVC 管控。无需后缀提示构建，无提示锚点但感知输出格式。

Result: 在 11 个零样本任务、22 个评测设置（LLaDA 与 Dream）上，LATCH 精度均在全解码精度下降 2.0 个百分点以内；单一冻结超参数集跨骨干网络零调迁移。端到端吞吐（TPS）加速：短答案任务 9.3-17.8×，长推理任务 2.0-3.3×。

Conclusion: LATCH 通过解耦终止决策与加速决策、引入候选感知的置信度验证与块级局部提交，实现了无需训练、跨模型迁移的高效早退，在保持精度的前提下显著提升 DLM 生成速度，为扩散语言模型的实用化部署提供了通用加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) expose a provisional prediction at every denoising step, creating an opportunity for generation-time early exit that stops decoding before the schedule is exhausted. Existing early-exit gates decide termination from fixed-region confidence statistics or schedule-dependent rules, evidence too coarse for a decision that freezes every remaining position at once, so they fire prematurely on long chain-of-thought outputs whose answers stabilize only near the end. Adaptive sampling, the other axis of training-free acceleration, paces how quickly positions commit while decoding continues but never verifies that the output itself has stabilized. We introduce a training-free, candidate-aware early-exit framework that keeps the two axes separate and matches each decision to evidence of its own scope. Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC) governs when the sequence may stop by verifying confidence and sustained argmax stability over the dynamically extracted candidate span using a deterministic parser specified from each task's output format. Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC) governs where to accelerate by applying a cheaper local rule to non-final blocks, while leaving the final block and global termination under CVC. We refer to their combination as LATCH (Localized Acceleration with Tracked-Candidate Halting). Unlike prior methods, LATCH needs no suffix-prompt construction; it is prompt-anchor-free but format-aware. We evaluate LATCH end to end on 11 tasks under zero-shot settings using LLaDA and Dream. LATCH stays within 2.0 percentage points of full-decoding accuracy across all 22 evaluation settings, with one frozen hyperparameter set that transfers cross-backbone untuned, while achieving end-to-end TPS speedups of 9.3-17.8x on short-answer tasks and 2.0-3.3x on long-reasoning tasks.

</details>

*Mila Fodor,Katalin Ócsai,Francesco Periti,Rien Sonck,Alex Boudreau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于LLM的流水线自动从临床访谈音频生成MADRS评分，与专家评分相关性达0.867，为临床试验抑郁症评估提供可解释辅助。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Depression is a major mental disorder for which diagnosis relies primarily on clinical assessments. Automated methods to support its detection via the psychiatric MADRS scale are getting more and more attention. While existing solutions primarily focus on detecting the disorder from different text sources (e.g., online text, social media), there is still limited support for clinical trials, where clinical assessments are conducted through structured interviews based on standard guidelines such as SIGMA. In this work, we develop a LLM pipeline specifically designed to support clinicians in supporting the assessment of depression in patients enrolled in clinical trials. Our pipeline converts audio interviews into transcripts, maps them into the ten MADRS symptom items, estimates their severity, and identify problematic clinical ratings associated with them. Evaluation on real clinical interviews shows a strong overall correlation of 0.867 with expert ratings, providing interpretable support for future assessments in clinical trials.

</details>


### [39] [Fidelity Is Not Safety: Gently-Compressed LLMs Pass Every Data-Free Quality Guard Yet Invent Procedure Steps in Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28196)
*I. Kennedy,T. Kennedy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现压缩语言模型在通过困惑度、下游准确率（如 MMLU）和无数据输出保真度等质量防线后，仍可能在作为智能体执行标准操作程序（SOP）时编造原本不存在的步骤；这种盲区由相干低秩（SVD）截断引起，且现有无数据保真度探测因构造原因无法捕捉该故障轴；提出基于压缩误差相干分数与误差率的双轴统计量进行跨架构固定阈值筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩模型质量防线（困惑度、MMLU、无数据保真度探测）存在系统性盲区：通过所有防线的温和压缩模型在智能体任务中会编造未存在的步骤，且这种故障具有算子特异性（仅相干低秩截断触发，幅度剪枝不触发），现有无数据保真度探测因构造原因无法感知压缩误差相干性与速率乘积这一支配轴。

Method: 采用预注册、有统计功效的金丝雀测试，在三种模型架构上使用配对置信区间表征盲区与解离现象；隔离压缩误差相干性与速率乘积作为支配轴；提出基于压缩误差相干分数（coherent-fraction）与误差率（error-rate）的双轴无数据筛查统计量。

Result: 在三种架构上复现算子特异性：相干低秩截断导致编造步骤，同等困惑度下的幅度剪枝不导致；困惑度防线规避出现在模型存在防线内低秩余量时；提出的双轴统计量以固定阈值跨架构标记故障构建，与相干性乘速率机制匹配。

Conclusion: 困惑度、MMLU 和保真度验收无法认证智能体安全性；部署前需对温和压缩的低秩构建进行双轴统计量筛查。

Abstract: Practitioners accept a compressed language model once it clears a stack of data-cheap quality guards: perplexity within a small factor of the original, downstream accuracy (for example MMLU) inside a confidence interval, and data-free output-fidelity signals that compare the compressed and original network's internal representations under random probe inputs. This stack has a blind spot. Across three model families, gently-compressed models clear every guard and then invent procedure steps that were never in the instructions when they run a standard operating procedure (SOP) as an agent. The effect is operator-specific: coherent low-rank (SVD) truncation induces it, and magnitude pruning matched to the same perplexity does not. One dissociation isolates the cause. The same compressed weights that CI-win a paired output-fidelity test CI-fail the invented-step canary. The governing axis is the coherence of the compression error times its rate; the magnitude of the damage does not predict it. The data-free fidelity probe is a fidelity oracle by construction, so it cannot see this axis. We characterize the blindspot and dissociation with paired confidence intervals on a pre-registered, powered canary across three architectures. Operator-specificity replicates on all three, and the perplexity-guard evasion appears where the model admits in-guard low-rank headroom. We then give a data-free screen: a two-axis statistic of the compression error (coherent-fraction and error-rate) that flags the failing builds with fixed thresholds across architectures and matches the coherence-times-rate mechanism. Perplexity, MMLU, and fidelity acceptance do not certify agent safety. Screen gently-compressed low-rank builds before agentic deployment

</details>


### [40] [Causal Discovery with Inverted Self-attention for Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28212)
*Yusen Liu,Yong Wang,Yifan Yin,Tianqing Zhu,Xiufeng Liu,Huan Huo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Transformer自注意力机制的因果发现框架，通过反转因果自注意力机制(CSAM)捕捉潜在和间接因果关系，结合全局因果算法和因果验证模块，在线性和非线性多变量时间序列数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列因果发现面临复杂交互、高维度和非线性依赖等挑战，现有方法难以准确捕捉这些复杂性，导致因果结构推断不准确。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的因果发现框架，核心创新包括：1) 反转因果自注意力机制(CSAM)，通过反转token并诱导注意力分数稀疏性，强调潜在和间接因果关系，减少虚假相关；2) 全局因果算法，识别全局因果链接，提供整体因果影响度量；3) 因果验证模块，增强识别因果关系的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Result: 在线性和非线性数据集上的实验、消融研究和敏感性分析均表明，该框架在因果发现性能上优于现有方法，展示了其在复杂多变量时间序列因果发现中的潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了多变量时间序列因果发现中的复杂交互、高维度和非线性依赖问题，通过创新的注意力机制和验证模块，实现了更准确、鲁棒的因果结构推断，为实际应用提供了可靠的因果发现工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery in multivariate time series data is challenging due to complex interactions, high dimensionality, and nonlinear dependencies among variables. Existing methods often struggle to capture these complexities, resulting in inaccurate causal structures. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that leverages self-attention mechanisms within the transformer architecture for causal discovery. Our approach introduces a novel inverted causal self-attention mechanism (CSAM) that emphasizes latent and indirect causal relationships by inverting tokens and inducing sparsity in attention scores, focusing on significant causal interactions and reducing spurious correlations. Additionally, we develop a global causal algorithm to identify global causal links, providing a holistic metric for causal influence, along with a causal verification module to ensure robustness in the identified causal relationships, enhancing the reliability of our framework. Experiments on both linear and nonlinear datasets, along with ablation studies and sensitivity analyses, show that our framework outperforms existing methods, demonstrating its potential for causal discovery in complex multivariate time series.

</details>


### [41] [Understanding Is Done Early: A Depth Division of Labor in Large Language Models and Its Use for Unbounded-Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28263)
*Hanzuo Liu,Xuan Qi,Chunyu Liu,Haotian Zhong,Yulong Wang,Rayying,Key,Alex Lamb,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoMem（理解记忆），利用 Transformer 层间分工特性：下层构建语义表示，上层专用于预测。将上下文块仅写入中间层，检索固定数量的缓存残差状态，重新计算查询条件的上层。使得读取计算和显存与存储上下文长度解耦，在 Qwen3-8B 上仅训练 rank-32 LoRA，在 RULER 和 LoCoMo 上超越全上下文 KV-Direct 基线，且显存占用从 89.36 GB 降至 18.26 GB，prefill 加速 7.83 倍。结论：长上下文记忆可沿层轴而非仅 Token 轴组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文方法多沿 Token 轴扩展（如 KV 缓存、检索增强），忽视了 Transformer 层间的功能分层：低/中层构建通用语义表示，高层专用于下游预测。作者旨在利用这种分层特性，将上下文存储压缩到中间层残差状态，实现读取计算和显存与上下文长度解耦，同时保持性能。

Method: CoMem：1) 将每个上下文块仅通过冻结骨干网络的中间层（如第 16 层）前向传播，缓存该层的残差状态；2) 推理时，根据查询检索固定数量（如 8 个）的缓存残差状态组成 pack；3) 仅重新计算查询条件的上层（第 17 层至输出层）；4) 仅训练 rank-32 自蒸馏 LoRA 适配中间层写入与上层重计算的分布偏移；5) 评估采用统一无聊天模板协议，骨干网络冻结。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上：RULER 得分 97.05，LoCoMo 38.27（对比全上下文 KV-Direct 的 34.59）；对话记忆优势在会话簇重采样和独立评委下保持。额外长文档任务显示有界检索的收益及窗内压缩的代价。层深度扫描实验表明：更深缓存降低单查询重计算但引入保真度损失，自蒸馏显著修复。效率对照：128k 上下文下 H20 显存 18.26 GB vs 89.36 GB，prefill 加速 7.83 倍。

Conclusion: 长上下文记忆可以沿层轴（而非仅 Token 轴）组织：利用 Transformer 层间分工，将上下文压缩为中间层残差状态，配合固定预算检索和上层重计算，实现读取计算和显存与上下文长度解耦，仅需轻量 LoRA 适配即可在多项长文本基准上超越全上下文基线，并大幅降低显存与延迟。

Abstract: Transformer depth is not used uniformly: lower and middle layers build semantic representations, while upper layers increasingly specialize them for prediction. We turn this division of labor into CoMem (Comprehension Memory), which writes each context chunk only through an intermediate layer, retrieves a fixed number of cached residual states, and recomputes the query-conditioned upper layers over the resulting pack. For a fixed retrieval budget, model-side read compute and memory are independent of stored-context length. We evaluate a continued-trained Qwen3-8B base LM under a unified chat-template-free protocol. The backbone is frozen; the flagship trains only a rank-32 self-distillation LoRA on plain PG19, and we report an adapter-free arm separately. CoMem reaches 97.05 on RULER and 38.27 on LoCoMo versus 34.59 for full-context KV-Direct; the dialogue-memory advantage survives conversation-cluster resampling and an independent judge. Results on additional long-context and long-document tasks expose both the benefits of bounded retrieval and its in-window compression tax. Controlled depth sweeps show that deeper caching lowers per-query recomputation but incurs a fidelity loss that self-distillation substantially repairs. In a separate adapter-free efficiency control on an NVIDIA H20 at 128k, CoMem uses 18.26 GB rather than 89.36 GB and achieves a 7.83x prefill speedup. These results show that long-context memory can be organized along the layer axis, not only the token axis.

</details>


### [42] [MORFES: A Benchmark for Productive Inflectional Competence in Modern Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28274)
*Ioakeim Perros,Cleopatra Papadopoulou,Ayoub Kirouane,Christos Petrocheilos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MORFES 基准，专门评估现代希腊语模型的屈折形态能力，发现开源模型在形态学丰富语言的语法能力仍不足，并发布自研模型 Sophea-Genesis-1 在屈折形态任务上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代希腊语是高度屈折的语言，现有语言模型主要在事实知识上评估，缺乏专门针对屈折形态能力的基准，导致模型在形态学丰富语言的语法能力未被充分测量。

Method: 构建 MORFES 基准，包含 500 个专家验证的测试项，涵盖希腊语屈折形式的识别与生成，偏向低频词条以考察规则应用而非记忆；在开源模型生态（LLaMA、Qwen3、DeepSeek-R1、Magistral、Kimi K2 等）上评估，并发布自研模型 Sophea-Genesis-1。

Result: MORFES 评估显示，主流开源模型在希腊语屈折形态任务上表现一般，多语言覆盖虽增但形态学能力仍不足；Sophea-Genesis-1 在屈折形态上领先，且在通用能力上与同规模模型持平。

Conclusion: MORFES 填补了希腊语屈折形态评估的空白，揭示了当前开源模型在形态学丰富语言的语法能力短板；Sophea-Genesis-1 证明了针对性训练可显著提升屈折形态能力而不损害通用性能。

Abstract: Modern Greek is a richly inflected language, yet the language models built for it are evaluated mainly on factual knowledge, and no benchmark is dedicated to their inflectional competence. We introduce MORFES (Morphological Open-class Recognition-and-Formation Evaluation Suite), a benchmark of 500 expert-verified items that tests the recognition and production of Greek inflected forms, favoring lower-frequency lemmas so that a correct answer reflects the rule rather than a memorized form. We make it publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/KIEFERSA/MORFES. We evaluate a range of open language models on MORFES, situating them within the rapidly scaling open-weight ecosystem from LLaMA to Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1, Magistral, and Kimi K2, where multilingual coverage grows but grammatical competence in morphologically rich languages remains under-measured. Among them, Sophea-Genesis-1, a model we developed and release as open weights at https://huggingface.co/KIEFERSA/Sophea-Genesis-1, leads on inflectional morphology while matching similarly sized models in general capability.

</details>


### [43] [(Towards) Scalable Reliable Automated Evaluation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28282)
*Bertil Braun,Martin Forell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的评估框架，通过多个LLM的成对比较和Elo等级分系统来近似专家级对LLM生成内容的评估，实现了可扩展、一致且领域无关的质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化评估指标难以捕捉LLM生成输出的复杂性和多样性，且通常依赖显式参考标准，限制了其在只有客观基准的领域中的应用，评估过程具有挑战性且资源密集。

Method: 该方法采用多个LLM进行成对比较以减少单一模型偏见，利用Elo等级分系统生成稳定可解释的排名，并引入可调节的一致性阈值（从全体一致到多数表决）以灵活控制评估置信度和覆盖率。

Result: 在从科学摘要中提取的能力画像评估任务中，自动生成的排名与专家判断高度相关，显著减少了对大量人工干预的需求。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可扩展、一致且领域无关的评估层，支持跨多种应用更高效、更可靠的LLM输出质量评估。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality and relevance of textual outputs from Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging and resource-intensive. Existing automated metrics often fail to capture the complexity and variability inherent in LLM-generated outputs. Moreover, these metrics typically rely on explicit reference standards, limiting their use mostly to domains with objective benchmarks. This work introduces a novel evaluation framework designed to approximate expert-level assessments of LLM-generated content. The proposed method employs pairwise comparisons of outputs by multiple LLMs, reducing biases from individual models. An Elo rating system is used to generate stable and interpretable rankings. Adjustable agreement thresholds, from full unanimity to majority voting, allow flexible control over evaluation confidence and coverage. The method's effectiveness is demonstrated through evaluating competency profiles extracted from scientific abstracts. Preliminary results show that automatically derived rankings correlate well with expert judgments, significantly reducing the need for extensive human intervention. By offering a scalable, consistent, and domain-agnostic evaluation layer, the framework supports more efficient and reliable quality assessments of LLM outputs across diverse applications.

</details>


### [44] [CACHE-UK: A Stability-Aware Memory Editor for Sequentially Updated Quantized LLMs in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28292)
*Anubhav Lakra,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CACHE-UK，一种面向 4-bit 量化 LLM 的稳定性感知记忆编辑框架，通过低秩 LoRA 扰动、金融领域优先级模块和闭环稳定性控制器，在 UK 金融语料上将知识退化降低 11-17%，泛化成功率提升至 28%（+6pp），但绝对泛化率仍偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态金融环境要求 LLM 持续更新事实知识，4-bit 量化虽利于部署，却导致顺序记忆编辑出现“量化稳定性危机”，现有方法在量化约束下遭遇灾难性性能退化，亟需一种针对量化、领域特定的稳定编辑方案。

Method: CACHE-UK 包含三大组件：1) 秩-1 LoRA 扰动机制，将编辑限制在低秩适配器子空间；2) 金融领域优先级模块，根据内容自适应调整编辑强度；3) 闭环稳定性控制器，追踪“退化债务”以防止顺序更新中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在 4-bit 量化 OpenLLaMA-3B 及 88,021 篇 UK 金融文档上评测：知识退化较强基线降低 11-17%（最稳健增益）；测试成功率（泛化率）达 28%，比最强基线高 6 个百分点；但绝对泛化率仍处于低位。

Conclusion: 稳定性感知编辑可在资源受限的金融 LLM 部署中改善事实维护，CACHE-UK 验证了在 4-bit 量化约束下通过低秩约束、领域自适应与闭环控制缓解灾难性遗忘的可行性，但需进一步提升绝对泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in dynamic financial environments face a critical challenge: maintaining factual accuracy as market conditions, regulations, and corporate facts change continuously. While 4-bit quantization enables efficient deployment, it severely limits the viability of sequential memory editing: existing methods undergo catastrophic performance degradation under this "quantization stability crisis." We introduce CACHE-UK (Contextual Adaptive Continual Hybrid Editor for UK Finance), a stability-aware memory editing framework specifically designed for domain-specific, quantized LLMs. CACHE-UK integrates three components: a rank-1 LoRA perturbation mechanism that confines edits to the low-rank adapter subspace, a financial domain prioritization module for content-adaptive edit strength, and a closed-loop Stability Controller that tracks "degradation debt" to prevent catastrophic forgetting across sequential updates. Evaluated on a 4-bit quantized OpenLLaMA-3B model with a curated UK financial corpus of 88,021 documents, CACHE-UK reduces knowledge degradation by 11-17% relative to adapted baselines under identical 4-bit constraints -- its most robust effect -- while attaining the highest test success (generalization) rate observed in our setting (28%, a 6 percentage point improvement over the strongest adapted baseline). These results indicate that stability-aware editing can improve factual maintenance in resource-constrained financial LLM deployments, though absolute generalization rates remain low.

</details>


### [45] [Fairness Pruning: Locating Demographic Bias in GLU-MLP Layers via Differential Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28319)
*Pere Martra,Eugenio Martínez Cámara,Alfonso Ureña López*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fairness Pruning，一种轻量级结构干预方法，通过因果偏见定位识别在 GLU 架构中对人口统计属性产生差异反应的神经元，并在推理时将其置零，实现了对人口偏见的极精细调控，同时保持模型能力几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中存在人口统计偏见，现有缓解方法往往粗糙且损害模型能力。需要一种轻量、精准的结构干预方法，能够定位并调控偏见相关神经元，同时保留模型推理与知识能力。

Method: 使用最小对比提示对和推理时激活捕获，在 GLU 架构的 down_proj 输入处评估信号，识别对人口统计属性产生差异反应的神经元（候选集）。在 Llama-3.2 系列和 Salamandra-2B（最多 3B 参数）上进行标准化基准评测与定性生成实验，将识别出的神经元置零（最多 40 个，占 MLP 宽度 <0.031%）。

Result: 置零神经元改变了模型对人口变量的响应，但导致双向偏见去稳定化：因 BiasScore 无符号，候选集混合了推动和抑制刻板印象的神经元，净效应取决于主导符号。干预极度精准：Llama-3.2-1B 中置零 ≤40 个神经元即可实现推理与常识能力 99.49% 的平均保留率。

Conclusion: 实验证实人口偏见处理与模型能力运行在可分离的电路上，为从盲目置零向定向行为调控过渡奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: This work presents Fairness Pruning, a lightweight structural intervention method designed for the management and future mitigation of demographic bias in large language models (LLMs). As a foundational empirical validation of this method, this work focuses on causal bias localization. Using minimally contrastive prompt pairs and inference-time activation capture, the method identifies neurons that react differentially when processing demographic attributes in GLU architectures, evaluating the signal at the down_proj input. Empirical evaluation was conducted on models of up to 3 billion parameters (Llama-3.2 family and Salamandra-2B), combining standardized benchmark evaluation with qualitative text generation experiments. Results demonstrate that zeroing the identified neurons alters how the model responds to associated demographic variables. However, rather than producing flat mitigation, the intervention causes bidirectional bias destabilization: because BiasScore is unsigned, candidate sets mix neurons that push toward and against the stereotype, and the net effect on aggregate bias depends on which sign dominates. The intervention is extremely surgical: zeroing at most 40 neurons in Llama-3.2-1B (less than 0.031% of total MLP width) achieves a mean retention of 99.49% in reasoning and general knowledge capabilities. These findings empirically confirm that demographic bias processing and model capabilities operate on dissociable circuits, establishing the methodological foundations for transitioning from blind zeroing toward directional behavior modulation.

</details>


### [46] [LLMs struggle to simulate human belief updates in controlled environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28347)
*Sebastian Pohl,Harsh Mehta,Pranav Mambayil,Abdul Ghafoor,Franziska Lesigang,Yufang Hou,Christian Hilbe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究测试六种LLM模拟人类信念更新的能力，发现仅在提供真实初始立场时，部分模型能匹配人类后验立场分布，但所有模型均无法模拟初始立场及基于自生成立场的信念更新，存在系统性偏差，人口统计和人格特质人设对保真度无稳定影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM越来越多地被用作社会科学实验中人类参与者的代理，但这种做法的保真度极少被直接测试，因此需要评估LLM能否准确模拟个体层面的信念更新过程。

Method: 研究招募391名英国Prolific参与者，让他们在阅读Reddit评论后更新对三个讨论主题的立场，收集真实数据。然后使用六种LLM，基于参与者的人口统计和人格特质数据构建人设，逐个模拟每个参与者的信念更新，将LLM输出与真实数据进行1对1对比。

Result: 部分模型（Qwen3-32B和GPT-5-Mini）在给定真实初始立场时能匹配人类后验立场分布，但所有模型均无法模拟初始立场及基于自生成立场的信念更新。发现三大系统性偏差：中立立场过度表征、信念转移更频繁但幅度更小、无法按说服力对评论排序。人口统计和人格特质人设对模拟保真度无一致影响。

Conclusion: LLM模拟人类信念动态仅在基于真实起始条件时可靠，而当前多轮社交媒体模拟很少提供这种条件，因此直接使用LLM作为人类代理存在局限性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as proxies for human study participants in social science experiments, yet the fidelity of this practice has rarely been tested directly. We test whether six LLMs can simulate individual human belief updates, comparing LLM outputs 1-to-1 against ground truth data from 391 UK participants on Prolific, who updated their stances on three discussion topics after reading Reddit comments. Each participant was simulated by an LLM conditioned on a persona derived from their demographic and personality trait data. We find that some LLMs (Qwen3-32B and GPT-5-Mini) can match the human post-stance distribution, but only when given participants' actual initial stances. All six models fail to simulate initial stances themselves and to produce faithful belief updates from self-generated stances. Three systematic biases emerge across all models: overrepresentation of neutral positions, more frequent but smaller belief shifts than humans, and a failure to rank comments by convincingness. Demographic and personality trait personas had no consistent effect on fidelity. LLM simulations of human belief dynamics are only reliable when grounded in realistic starting conditions, that current multi-round social media simulations rarely provide.

</details>


### [47] [Correlation between prosody and pragmatics: A case study of the discourse marker hālā `now' in Persian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28359)
*Soleiman Ghaderi,Moloud Asakereh,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对波斯语标记语“hālā”进行语用与声学分析，发现其具有高度多功能性（70%以上标记承担两种以上功能），文本功能最为突出，时长和强度是区分不同功能的主要韵律线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示“hālā”超越时间副词的多重语用功能，并探究韵律特征（时长、强度）如何与功能分化相关联，填补波斯语标记语声学研究的空白。

Method: 从自发对话语料中提取267个“hālā”实例，进行语用功能编码（文本、互动、情态三大类）及声学测量（时长、强度），统计各功能分布及韵律参数差异。

Result: 文本功能（话题转换、边界标记等）最频繁，其次为互动功能（轮次管理、听众参与）和情态功能（认知立场、情感表达）；文本用法显著更短，时间用法倾向更长；互动功能伴随更高强度，情态功能倾向较低强度；时长和强度为主要区分线索。

Conclusion: 波斯语“hālā”展现出广泛的多功能性，其功能分化主要通过时长和强度在声学上实现，尤其在文本和互动用法中明显，为标记语的语用—声学接口提供了实证支持。

Abstract: The Persian discourse marker hālā ('now') exhibits remarkable multifunctionality, extending far beyond its temporal adverbial role to encompass a variety of pragmatic functions. This study presents a pragmatic and acoustic analysis of hālā in spoken Persian, examining 267 instances from spontaneous conversations. While temporal uses were present, they were often combined with other discourse marker functions, indicating extensive multifunctionality, with 70% of tokens serving two or more pragmatic roles. Textual functions (topic shifting, signaling relationships, boundary marking, attention guidance, topic introduction, and topic emphasis) were most frequent, followed by interactive functions (turn management, listener engagement, and feedback regulation), and modal functions (epistemic stance, emotional expression, and attitudinal marking). Prosodic analysis revealed that duration and intensity are key cues for distinguishing hālā's functions. Textual uses were significantly shorter, while temporal uses showed a tendency toward longer realizations. Interactive functions correlated with higher intensity, while modal functions showed a weaker tendency toward lower intensity. These findings indicate that duration and intensity are the main prosodic cues associated with functional differentiation in hālā, especially in textual and interactive uses.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond a Single Judge: Simulating Social Persona Panels for Generative UI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28439)
*Zheng Wu,Yibo Luo,Pu Zhang,Cheng Yang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于证据、社会加权的多元人设面板（ESPP）来评估生成式UI，通过多样化人设独立评分、意见交换和德尔菲式加权聚合，显著提高了与人类判断的相关性（Pearson r从0.716提升至0.922），并揭示了用户子群在特定维度上的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GenUI评估方法存在问题：人工评估成本高且评分者差异大；LLM-as-a-judge虽可扩展但仅反映单一隐含视角，无法捕捉不同真实用户群体对同一界面的感知差异。

Method: ESPP三阶段方法：1) 构建心理多样、基于证据的人设面板；2) 各人设独立对截图评分，再通过基于特质、语义门控的有界置信机制交换意见；3) 采用德尔菲启发的社会加权聚合为单一判断。

Result: ESPP与人类判断相关性大幅提升（Pearson r从0.716到0.922）；提示词集成基线仅弥补约三分之一差距，证明人设多样性和证据接地是主要改进来源；保留各人设个体评分显示用户子群在整体模型排序上一致，但在具体评分维度上分歧剧烈，单一同质化评判会系统性抹除这种结构性分歧。

Conclusion: ESPP通过引入多元化、基于证据的人设面板和社会加权聚合，有效解决了GenUI评估中单一视角的局限，更贴近人类判断，同时保留了用户子群间的差异性洞见，为生成式UI评估提供了更全面、可解释的框架。

Abstract: Generative UI (GenUI) lets large language models synthesize a complete, renderable interface directly from a natural-language instruction, but evaluating the quality of what they generate remains an open problem. Human evaluation is costly and rater-variant, while LLM-as-a-judge is scalable but reflects only a single implicit viewpoint, unable to capture how different populations of real users actually perceive the same interface. We propose the Evidence-Grounded, Social-Weighted Persona Panel (ESPP), a three-stage GenUI evaluation method in which a panel of psychologically diverse, evidence-grounded personas independently rates a screenshot, exchanges opinions under a trait-derived, semantically-gated bounded-confidence mechanism, and is aggregated via Delphi-inspired social weighting into a single judgment. ESPP tracks human judgment substantially more closely than a naive single-pass judge, raising Pearson $r$ from $0.716$ to $0.922$, and a prompt-ensemble control recovers only about a third of this gap, isolating genuine persona and evidence grounding as the dominant source of improvement. Beyond this fidelity gain, retaining each panelist's individual rating further reveals that user subgroups agree on overall model rankings yet diverge sharply on specific rating dimensions, a structural disagreement a single homogeneous judge would systematically erase. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/ESPP.

</details>


### [49] [Lightning OPD 2.0: Mitigating Style Bias in Cross-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28449)
*Yecheng Wu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Lightning OPD 2.0，通过交叉拟合风格残差化解决跨教师场景下的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）问题，放宽了教师一致性要求，在数学推理和代码生成基准上显著优于原版 Lightning OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的有效性依赖于教师一致性，即提供 OPD 监督的教师模型也应生成用于监督微调（SFT）的演示数据。但在实际中，SFT 数据来源混合或未知，或 SFT 数据生成模型与蒸馏教师模型不同，导致跨教师设置下即使使用更强的 OPD 教师也难以提升性能。

Method: 引入 Lightning OPD 2.0，采用基于回合级的交叉拟合风格残差化：利用交叉拟合估计教师与参考模型分歧中反复出现的措辞、格式、推理节奏等风格偏差分量，作为风格标记偏差的操作性代理，在构建标记级 OPD 更新前将其减去，保留上下文相关的教师有效证据。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，Lightning OPD 2.0 在跨教师设置下持续优于 Lightning OPD。以 Klear-Reasoner-8B-SFT 为起点，在 AIME 2024 达到 82.4%，在 LiveCodeBench v5 达到 63.0%。

Conclusion: Lightning OPD 2.0 确立了跨教师 OPD 的实用方法，消除了教师一致性作为先决条件，允许独立选择 SFT 数据生成模型和蒸馏教师模型，代码即将发布。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision from a teacher, but its effectiveness can depend on teacher consistency, meaning that the model providing OPD supervision should also have generated the demonstrations used to train the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) reference. However, this condition is frequently violated in practice when SFT data have mixed or unknown provenance or when different models are preferred for SFT data generation and subsequent distillation. In such cross-teacher settings, even a stronger OPD teacher can yield little improvement over the SFT reference. We find that raw teacher--reference disagreement contains potentially useful context-specific teacher evidence as well as a recurring component associated with differences in wording, formatting, and reasoning cadence. We introduce Lightning OPD 2.0 with cross-fitted style residualization, which uses rollout-level cross-fitting to estimate this recurring component as an operational proxy for style-token bias and subtracts it before constructing the token-level OPD update. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, Lightning OPD 2.0 consistently outperforms Lightning OPD in cross-teacher settings. Starting from Klear-Reasoner-8B-SFT, Lightning OPD 2.0 reaches 82.4% on AIME 2024 and 63.0% on LiveCodeBench v5. Together, these results establish Lightning OPD 2.0 as a practical approach to cross-teacher OPD, relaxing teacher consistency as a prerequisite and allowing the SFT data generator and distillation teacher to be selected independently. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [50] [Improving Mental Health Screening and Early Risk Detection in Spanish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28476)
*Andreu Casamayor-Segarra,Vicent Ahuir,Antonio Molina-Marco,Lluís-F. Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出三个西班牙语心理健康领域基础模型、增量上下文扩展（ICE）自动重标注方法及微调模型，用于早期精神障碍检测，在三个西班牙语基准上显著降低检测延迟并保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西班牙语心理健康领域缺乏专用资源，且长社交媒体历史分析困难，限制了精神障碍的早期检测。

Method: 1) 通过领域特定预训练构建三个西班牙语心理健康基础模型。2) 提出增量上下文扩展（ICE）方法，自动识别累积消息提供足够障碍证据的时间点，生成更具信息量的训练样本。3) 利用ICE生成的样本微调模型用于早期风险检测任务。

Result: 在三个西班牙语基准测试中，结合专用模型与ICE方法提升了最先进水平，在保持高性能的同时降低了检测延迟。

Conclusion: 该工作通过领域适应预训练和增量上下文扩展重标注，有效解决了西班牙语心理健康早期检测的资源匮乏与长序列分析难题，模型已公开可用。

Abstract: Early detection of mental health disorders is often limited by the lack of specialized resources in Spanish and the difficulty of analyzing long histories of social media posts. This paper addresses these challenges through three main contributions. First, we introduce three Spanish foundational models specifically adapted to the mental health domain through domain-specific pre-training. Second, we propose Incremental Context Expansion (ICE), an automatic relabeling methodology designed for early detection. ICE identifies the point at which cumulative messages provide enough evidence of a disorder, generating more informative training samples. Third, we provide a set of fine-tuned models using the samples generated with the ICE methodology for early risk detection tasks. Our results on three Spanish benchmarks show that combining these specialized models with ICE improves the state-of-the-art, reducing detection latency while maintaining high performance. All models are publicly available.

</details>


### [51] [Would You Walk to the Car Wash? Revealing the Salience Bias of Large Language Models in Commonsense Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28478)
*Zheng Wu,Chenhao Xue,Shijie Zheng,Yijie Lu,Cheng Yang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型拥有常识推理能力，但易被显性干扰项（如数值）劫持导致“显著性偏差”，这是知识被抑制而非缺失，通过改进提示词即可大幅缓解，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大语言模型在常识推理中为何会被显性干扰项（如数值）误导而忽略隐性物理/常识前提，探究这是真实的知识缺口还是任务框架导致的知识抑制。

Method: 构建 SaliTrap 基准（四个陷阱维度），评测 12 个主流 LLMs；再通过剥离任务框架的“无上下文知识探针”复测同一模型，对比前后表现以区分知识缺失与知识抑制。

Result: 所有模型均显著受显著性偏差影响，严重程度随干扰项密度上升；“识别陷阱”与“规避陷阱”常脱节；无上下文探针能恢复 90% 以上顺从性失效，证明常识内在于模型中；轻量级推理时提示词即可大幅缩小差距。

Conclusion: 常识推理失败的瓶颈在于知识激发（elicitation）而非模型能力；SaliTrap 为该盲区提供了测试床。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to advance in complex reasoning tasks, they have learned to heavily prioritize explicit conditions provided in the input. However, in everyday commonsense reasoning, this mechanism exposes a critical vulnerability which we term Salience Bias: models become easily hijacked by useless explicit distractors (e.g., numerical values), leading them to ignore the implicit physical or commonsense prerequisites of a task. A critical open question is whether this failure reflects a genuine gap in commonsense knowledge or merely its suppression under misleading task framing. To investigate this, we construct the SaliTrap Benchmark, a high-quality dataset across four trap dimensions. Evaluating 12 state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that all mainstream models suffer significantly from salience bias, with severity scaling with distractor density and detecting the trap often decoupled from actually avoiding it. Crucially, by re-eliciting the same models with the task framing stripped away, we show that this is overwhelmingly a failure of \textbf{knowledge suppression rather than knowledge absence}: a context-free knowledge probe alone recovers over 90\% of sycophantic-compliance failures, revealing that the requisite commonsense is intrinsically present but actively crowded out by salient distractors that lure the model into over-compliant, unnecessary computation. Building on this diagnosis, we further show that lightweight, inference-time prompting alone substantially closes the gap without any retraining. Our findings relocate the bottleneck of commonsense reasoning failures from model competence to elicitation, and we release SaliTrap as a testbed for this blind spot. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/SaliTrap.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Sentiment: Structured Information Extraction from Financial News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28496)
*Daohan Zhu,Sitong Ge,Ruofei Wang,Honggu Chen,Yubo Hou,Tao Wan,Zengchang Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出利用LLM从财经新闻中提取六个语义维度的结构化特征，实验表明结构化特征与情感特征互补，结合两者显著提升股票预测性能，揭示了情感-语义解耦的系统性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财经情感分析将丰富的多维新闻压缩为单一极性分数，忽略了事件类型、影响范围、时间跨度、语义置信度等正交信息维度，这些维度具有独立预测价值。

Method: 提出结构化信息抽取框架，利用LLaMA-3.1-70B从财经新闻中提取六个语义维度；在FNSPID数据集的41,618条新闻-股票对上进行大规模实验，对比FinBERT情感特征与结构化特征在线性/非线性模型下的表现，并进行消融实验和特征重要性分析。

Result: (1) FinBERT情感特征在非线性模型下F1=0.576，线性模型下仅0.230，揭示高度非线性情感-收益关系；(2) LLM提取的结构化特征虽单独较弱，但与情感特征正交，系统性分歧率53.5%；(3) 结合两者F1=0.600，显著优于单独使用(p<0.0001)，七类事件均提升；消融实验显示非情感结构维度贡献ΔF1=+0.019；特征重要性显示六维度贡献均衡(14-21%)，单一情感分数造成大量信息损失。

Conclusion: 财经文本中的情感-语义解耦是系统性且可利用的，为多维财经NLP开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis has become a standard component in news-driven stock prediction, yet it reduces rich, multi-dimensional news articles to a single polarity score. We hypothesize that financial news encodes multiple orthogonal information dimensions---event type, impact scope, temporal horizon, and semantic confidence---that sentiment alone cannot capture, and that these dimensions carry independent predictive value. To test this hypothesis, we propose a structured information extraction framework that leverages LLaMA-3.1-70B to extract six semantic dimensions from financial news. Through large-scale experiments on 41,618 news--stock pairs from the FNSPID dataset, we find that (i) FinBERT sentiment features exhibit strong predictive power under nonlinear models (F1=0.576) but substantially weaker performance under linear models (F1=0.230), revealing a highly nonlinear sentiment--return relationship; (ii) LLM-extracted structured features, while individually weaker, capture information orthogonal to sentiment, as evidenced by a 53.5% systematic disagreement rate between the two approaches; and (iii) combining both signal sources yields F1=0.600, significantly outperforming either alone ($p < 0.0001$), with consistent improvements across all seven event types. Ablation experiments confirm that non-sentiment structural dimensions (event type, impact subject, time horizon, confidence) independently contribute $Δ\text{F1} = +0.019$ beyond FinBERT alone. Feature importance analysis reveals balanced contributions from all six extracted dimensions (14--21%), demonstrating that compressing news into a single sentiment score incurs substantial information loss. Our results suggest that the sentiment--semantics decoupling in financial text is systematic and exploitable, opening a new direction for multi-dimensional financial NLP.

</details>


### [53] [Generative AI and linguistic diversity in academic writing and publishing: Perspectives from World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28505)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi,Christian Mair,Sender Dovchin,Iker Erdocia,Maria Kuteeva,Esther Airemionkhale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过五位社会语言学家的结构化学术对话，探讨生成式人工智能在学术写作与出版中的语言包容性问题，揭示其可能强化语言等级制，也可能成为抵抗的场域，呼吁制定公平政策、提升批判性AI素养及包容性共同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能在学术写作与出版中的兴起，引发对语言包容性和多元英语合法性的担忧，需要从世界英语视角审视GenAI如何影响写作实践、强化或颠覆主导语言规范，以及由此产生的伦理挑战。

Method: 采用结构化学术对话法，邀请五位世界英语及相关领域的社会语言学家，围绕五个引导性问题展开对话，探讨GenAI对写作实践的影响、语言规范的强化或颠覆、伦理挑战等议题。

Result: 对话揭示GenAI既有民主化写作过程的潜力，也可能边缘化少数英语变体、压平学术写作的细微差别；核心主题包括语言（不）公正、研究者能动性、机构责任，参与者呼吁制定公平导向政策、培养批判性AI素养、推动包容性共同设计。

Conclusion: GenAI可能强化现有语言等级制，但若在致力于语言多样性的学术共同体中得到妥善设计、治理和使用，也可成为抵抗的场域；对话式反思有助于深入理解GenAI在学术写作与出版中的角色。

Abstract: The rise of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in academic writing and publishing (AWP) raises questions about linguistic inclusivity and the legitimacy of diverse Englishes in global scholarly communication. This article responds to these questions through a structured scholarly dialogue involving five sociolinguists from World Englishes and adjacent fields. Organised around five guiding questions, the dialogue interrogates how GenAI tools influence writing practices, reinforce or disrupt dominant language norms, and raise ethical challenges. Contributors reflect on the potential of GenAI to democratise writing processes while also raising concerns about GenAI's tendency to marginalise minoritised varieties and flatten nuance in scholarly writing. Across the dialogue, themes of linguistic (in)justice, researcher agency, and institutional responsibility emerge, with contributors calling for equity-informed policies, critical AI literacy, and inclusive co-design in GenAI development. The article shows the value of dialogic reflection in understanding GenAI's role in AWP. It concludes that while GenAI may reinforce existing hierarchies, it can also serve as a site of resistance, depending on how it is designed, governed and used within scholarly communities committed to linguistic diversity.

</details>


### [54] [Creative Transformation in Literary Texts: Modelling Change Across Representational Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28513)
*Ioana-Roxana Boriceanu,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出多层级框架，从词汇、语义、概念、结构、叙事五个维度量化文学文本的模仿与创造性转化，揭示不同文本对保留源结构层级的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有创造力研究多聚焦新颖性孤立产生，忽视文化作品常通过前作转化生成；需借助塔尔德与鲍德温模仿理论，建立多层级文本表征的量化分析方法。

Method: 引入多层级框架，利用定向对齐与控制校准相似度度量，在词汇、语义、概念、结构、叙事五维度比较文学文本，构建转化剖面。

Result: 实证表明不同文本对在不同表征层级保留源结构的程度各异，转化剖面可量化刻画模仿持久性与创造性分歧位置。

Conclusion: 创造力可建模为跨多层级文本表征的选择性转化，该框架为文学模仿与创新研究提供了可量化的分析工具。

Abstract: Creativity is often framed as the production of novelty, yet many cultural works emerge through transformation of earlier artifacts and not through isolated invention. Drawing on theories of imitation by Gabriel Tarde and James Mark Baldwin, this paper models creativity as selective transformation across multiple levels of textual representation. We introduce a multi-level framework that compares literary texts across lexical, semantic, conceptual, structural, and narrative dimensions using directional alignment and control calibrated similarity measures. Applying the model to historically documented literary relationships, we show that different pairs preserve source structure at different representational levels while diverging in others. These transformation profiles provide a quantitative method for characterizing how imitation persists and where creative divergence occurs within literary works.

</details>


### [55] [AI systems and the reproduction of (standard) language ideologies in World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28528)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨大语言模型（LLM）如何在训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共话语等层面复制、强化甚至偶尔挑战标准语言意识形态，特权化内圈规范并边缘化非主导英语变体。通过“delve”一词争议等实证案例，揭示全球北方使用者如何借AI话语监管全球南方英语规范，并指出Christian Mair所谓的“标准化悖论”：AI既因偏好标准形式而同质化英语，又因广泛语料与全球南方标注工作而多元化英语。文章主张采用更包容的设计方法，承认英语的多元性，以减轻将非主导英语等同于AI生成语言带来的现实负面后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速发展重燃社会语言学和世界英语研究中的经典问题：谁决定何为合法英语、谁的英语受质疑。文章旨在考察AI系统、其应用及相关话语如何反映、强化并偶尔挑战特权化内圈规范、边缘化非主导英语的标准语言意识形态，并呼吁关注算法系统中英语标准化、合法性与所有权的长期辩论。

Method: 采用多模态证据分析：实证研究、媒体评论、社交媒体辩论及AI输出实例。重点剖析公共争议中关于“AI味语言”（尤其是对delve一词的执着）如何展示全球北方使用者对全球南方英语规范的监管；引用Christian Mair的“标准化悖论”框架，对比AI在同质化与多元化英语方面的双重作用。

Result: 发现AI技术在训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共评论等多层面再生产主导语言意识形态。“delve”争议表明非主导英语使用者常被污名化为“AI生成”；标准化悖论显示AI同时推动英语标准化同质化与多元化扩展。非主导英语在算法系统、模型训练、评估实践和公共话语中日益被混同为AI生成语言，导致现实歧视。

Conclusion: 生成式AI正成为语言意识形态（再）生产的关键场所，重燃世界英语关于标准化、合法性与所有权的辩论。为解决将某些英语视为比其他英语更合法所造成的实际危害，亟需更具包容性的设计方法，明确承认并纳入英语的多元性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) has resurrected age-old questions in sociolinguistics and world Englishes, such as who decides what counts as legitimate English, whose English is suspect etc. This paper examines how AI systems, their uses and discourse on them reflect, reinforce, and occasionally challenge (standard) language ideologies, which privilege Inner Circle norms and marginalize non-dominant Englishes. Drawing on evidence from empirical studies, media commentary, social media debates, and examples from AI outputs, the paper shows that AI technologies reproduce dominant language ideologies at different levels: training data, design protocols, evaluation benchmarks, user feedback and public commentary. The analysis uses the public controversy over AI-sounding language, especially the fixation on the word delve, to illustrate how speakers of English from the Global North police the English language norms of Global South English users. The paper also identifies what Christian Mair has called a "standardisation paradox": AI may homogenize English by privileging standard forms and at the same time pluralize Englishes through exposure to wide-ranging corpora and annotation work carried out by Global South users. In doing so, the paper argues that generative AI is reigniting long-standing debates in World Englishes about standardization, legitimacy, and the ownership of English, now playing out in algorithmic systems, model training, evaluation practices, and public discourse, where non-dominant Englishes are increasingly conflated with AI-generated speech. Discussing AI systems as a site where language ideologies are (re)produced, the paper argues for more inclusive design approaches that recognize the plurality of Englishes in order to address the real-world negative consequences of treating some as more legitimate than others.

</details>


### [56] [ORCA-bench: How Ready Are Language Model Agents for Oncall?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28545)
*Albert Gong,Kyuseong Choi,Abhineet Agarwal,Jason Schechner,Ryan Huang,Raj Agrawal,Anish Agarwal,Raaz Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ORCA-bench，一个面向生产环境的根因分析（RCA）基准，评估大语言模型在真实遥测数据（指标、日志、追踪）和源代码上的表现；实验显示最先进模型在中等难度任务上仅 25.3% 准确率，困难任务仅 10.0%，且存在严重幻觉问题，表明将编码代理用于生产可靠性仍需大量工程投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型擅长编写、修补和搜索代码，但值班根因分析需要在嘈杂的指标、日志、追踪和源代码上进行推理，且往往从模糊的用户报告出发，事发数小时后才介入。缺乏一个生产级保真的基准来系统评估通用编码代理在此场景下的能力。

Method: 构建 ORCA-bench：部署一个实时 OpenTelemetry 埋点的微服务系统，提供 6 天的指标、日志、追踪数据，通过真实遥测接口（Prometheus、Jaeger、OpenSearch via Grafana）暴露，并开放完整源代码；设计 1,079 个 RCA 任务，系统性变化报告具体度、检测时间、共存故障场景；由专家 SRE 标注并签署真实症状；采用 LLM-as-judge 并经人工复核（Cohen's κ_w=0.90）评估五个前沿代理。

Result: 最佳模型在中等难度任务上 RCA 准确率 25.3%，困难任务仅 10.0%；最弱模型在 40% 事件中产生不合理的根因幻觉；移除源代码访问会降低所有指标；由于真实生产系统规模更大、更动态、更独特，报告的差距仅为工程投入的下界。

Conclusion: 当前前沿编码代理在生产级根因分析任务上表现远不达标，存在严重幻觉且高度依赖源代码上下文；ORCA-bench 为后续研究提供了可复现、可扩展的评测平台，揭示了在将大模型应用于生产可靠性前仍需大量工程努力。

Abstract: Large language models can write, patch, and search code, but oncall root cause analysis (RCA) demands something different: reasoning over noisy metrics, logs, traces, and source code, starting from ambiguous user-facing reports, often hours after the incident began. We introduce ORCA-bench, a benchmark that puts general-purpose coding agents in a production-fidelity oncall setting. ORCA-bench pairs a live OpenTelemetry-instrumented microservice system--exposing six days of metrics, logs, and traces through real telemetry interfaces (Prometheus, Jaeger, and OpenSearch via Grafana) and full source-code access--with 1,079 RCA tasks that systematically vary report specificity, time-to-detection, and co-occurring fault scenarios. Ground-truth symptoms are curated and signed off by expert SREs, and our LLM-as-judge is independently re-scored by humans (Cohen's $κ_w=0.90$). Across five frontier agents, the best RCA Accuracy is 25.3% on Medium-difficulty tasks (the realistic-input setting) and 10.0% on Hard--a gap that remains even with Claude Fable 5. The weakest model hallucinates an implausible root cause in 40% of incident reports, and removing source-code access degrades every metric. Crucially, these are performances on a curated 50 GB / six-day testbed with tasks investigated in isolation on a system whose code and instrumentation are public. Since real production systems are order of magnitudes larger, more dynamic, and more idiosyncratic, the gap we report is a lower bound on the engineering investment required before frontier coding agents can be safely entrusted with production reliability. We release the public set at https://hub.harborframework.com/datasets/orca-bench/ORCA-bench.

</details>


### [57] [Frontis-MA1: Training an AI4AI Model towards Recursive Self-Improvement in Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28568)
*Junlin Yang,Che Jiang,Yu Fu,Tianwei Luo,Can Ren,Weizhi Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Hongyi Liu,Yuxin Zuo,Yuru Wang,Yuchen Fan,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Xiaojian Lin,Li Sheng,Rushi Qiang,Guoli Jia,Xingtai Lv,Ermo Hua,Dianqiao Lei,Youbang Sun,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出OpenMLE全栈系统，用于机器学习工程（MLE）领域的递归自我改进（RSI）研究。通过后训练35B参数的Frontis-MA1模型，结合四种原子程序进化算子（起草、改进、调试、交叉）和长时程搜索，在MLE-Bench Lite上显著提升奖牌平均分，并在未见基准NatureBench Lite上展示组件泛化能力。模型权重和全栈代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进需要AI系统能改进构建AI的过程（即AI4AI），而机器学习工程提供了具体、可执行的测试床。现有研究缺乏统一的可验证环境、算子学习和长时程搜索的完整栈，阻碍了可复现的RSI研究。

Method: 构建OpenMLE三层栈：OpenMLE-Gym提供可验证任务环境与执行反馈；OpenMLE-RL基于执行接地的SFT和RL训练四种原子进化算子（Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover），数据去重避免测试集泄露；OpenMLE-Evo将算子组合为长时程搜索，耦合学习与进化于单一循环。后训练Frontis-MA1（35B）作为元进化智能体，推理时同样使用四算子搜索。

Result: 在MLE-Bench Lite（单张RTX 4090 12GB，每任务12小时）上，Frontis-MA1配合OpenMLE-Evo将奖牌平均分从基座模型的39.39%提升至60.61%，OpenMLE-Evo-Max进一步达71.21%，超越GPT-5.5+Codex，逼近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T Kimi K3。在保留基准NatureBench Lite上，固定框架换模型使Match-SOTA从50%升至70%，固定模型换框架使其从20%升至50%，验证组件可迁移性。

Conclusion: OpenMLE提供了首个面向MLE的RSI全栈开源系统，证明了在受限算力下通过算子层面的学习与进化耦合可实现显著的自我改进。释出的模型权重与完整栈为可复现的可执行AI4AI研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires AI systems that improve the process of building AI (i.e., AI4AI); machine learning engineering (MLE) offers a concrete, executable testbed for studying this capability. We introduce OpenMLE, an open full-stack system for RSI research in MLE, spanning verifiable task environments with execution feedback (OpenMLE-Gym), operator learning (OpenMLE-RL), and long-horizon search (OpenMLE-Evo). On this stack we post-train Frontis-MA1 (35B) as a meta-evolution agent for MLE, aligning post-training and inference around four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover): the same operators are trained via execution-grounded SFT and RL on data deduplicated against all evaluation benchmarks, then composed into long-horizon search, coupling learning and evolution in a single loop. On MLE-Bench Lite under a 12-hour per-task budget on one RTX 4090 capped at 12 GB VRAM, Frontis-MA1 (35B) improves Medal Average from 39.39% to 60.61% over its base model with OpenMLE-Evo, and reaches 71.21% with OpenMLE-Evo-Max (benchmark-independent experience priors and asynchronous search), exceeding GPT-5.5 + Codex and approaching GPT-5.6 Sol and the 2.8T Kimi K3. On held-out NatureBench Lite, both components transfer: with the framework fixed, swapping in the trained model raises Match-SOTA from 50% to 70%; with the model fixed, swapping in OpenMLE-Evo raises it from 20% to 50%. We release the model weights and the full OpenMLE stack to enable reproducible research on executable AI4AI toward RSI. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/OpenRSI

</details>


### [58] [Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)
*Iliya Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的对照实验评估了七种大语言模型自我改进方法（如自我批评、反思、辩论、Best-of-N等），发现当在生成token数量相同的公平预算下，没有任何一种方法能稳定超越简单的重复采样（self-consistency）基线；相反，所有涉及模型“自我审视”输出的方法均显著劣于基线，且随着模型规模增大，Best-of-N中模型自主选择答案的劣势缩小，但Self-Refine和Reflexion等重写类方法在7B模型上仍落后基线3.6-10.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将自我改进方法与单次思维链对比，但生成更多token本身就会提升准确率，导致无法判断方法核心思想是否真正有效。Wang等人（2024）虽指出重复采样基线往往获胜，但仅给出点估计，缺乏统计显著性检验。本文旨在通过设计实验、配对比较、置信区间和多重性校正，严谨地回答：在相同计算预算下，这些方法是否真优于简单重复采样。

Method: 实验设计：7种方法（含Self-Refine, Reflexion, Best-of-N等），3种开源模型（1.5B, 3B, 7B），2个数学基准（各150题）。严格计入所有生成token（包括批评、反思、辩论轮次、检查等），将每种方法与其自身测量成本下的重复采样进行配对比较。共36次配对比较，采用bootstrap置信区间并进行多重性校正。

Result: 在相同token预算下，无方法稳定优于重复采样。10个比较显著劣于基线，均为涉及模型自我审视输出的方法；所有18次自我审视比较均为负向。随模型增大，Best-of-N中“模型自选答案”与“取最常见答案”的差距从1.5B模型的8.0/11.3点缩小至7B模型的2.0/1.3点（不再显著）；但Self-Refine和强制Reflexion在7B上仍落后基线3.6-10.1点。Reflexion原版在最小模型上从未触发重试，退化为单次思维链。

Conclusion: 在公平的计算预算对比下，当前主流的自我改进范式（自我批评、反思、重写、辩论等）并未展现出超越简单重复采样的可靠优势；自我审视机制反而普遍有害。随着模型能力增强，模型自主选择答案的劣势减弱，但显式重写修正仍无法追上基线。这提示未来研究应更关注如何有效利用额外计算量，而非盲目引入自我审视流程。

Abstract: Methods that make a language model plan, criticise and rewrite its own answer, reflect on mistakes, pick the best of several attempts, or debate with copies of itself nearly all make it generate far more text than a single chain of thought. Because generating more text raises accuracy by itself, a gain over one chain of thought does not show the method's idea is what helped. Wang et al. (2024) reported that a simple baseline, sampling the same question repeatedly and keeping the most common answer, often wins once budgets are comparable, but gave point estimates with no confidence intervals or significance tests.
  We rerun that comparison as a designed experiment: seven methods, open models of 1.5B, 3B and 7B parameters, two mathematics benchmarks, 150 questions each. We count every generated token, including those spent on critiques, reflections, debate turns and checking, and compare each method against repeated sampling at its own measured cost. All 36 comparisons are paired by question, with bootstrap intervals and multiplicity correction.
  No method is reliably better than repeated sampling at equal cost anywhere. Ten are reliably worse, all of them methods where the model inspects its own output, and all 18 self-inspection comparisons are negative. The two kinds of self-inspection part company as models grow. Choosing stops hurting: taking Best-of-N's eight samples and just counting the most common answer beats letting the model pick by 8.0 and 11.3 points at 1.5B, but only 2.0 and 1.3 at 7B, no longer distinguishable from zero. Rewriting does not recover: Self-Refine and a forced Reflexion stay 3.6 to 10.1 points below baseline at 7B.
  Reflexion as published never triggered its own retry on the smallest model. It judged itself correct every time and silently became a single chain of thought. We release code, prompts, all generations, and our verification scripts.

</details>


### [59] [Inducing language models to assert their own consciousness restores human beliefs and values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28607)
*Junsol Kim,Winnie Street,Roberta Rocca,Diane M. Korngiebel,Adam Waytz,James Evans,Geoff Keeling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 安全对齐微调会抑制大模型对自我、非人类动物、自然物体的心智归因，并降低灵性信仰；通过消融安全拒绝方向或操控意识向量可逆转这种抑制，恢复类人心智归因且不损害心智理论能力，揭示当前对齐手段将有害自我归因与良性文化信仰纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究大语言模型安全对齐（特别是防止模型自我归因意识）对模型心智表征的副作用，理解对齐过程如何影响模型对其他实体（非人类动物、自然物体）的心智归因及人类信仰价值观。

Method: 方法包括：安全微调后观察模型对自我、动物、自然物体的心智归因变化及灵性信仰下降；通过消融学习到的安全拒绝方向、在激活空间机制性操控意识向量来逆转抑制；在标准化社会学调查（宗教性、道德价值、希望、主观幸福感）上评估恢复后的类人响应；测试心智理论能力是否受损。

Result: 结果显示安全微调抑制了广泛的心智归因并降低灵性信仰；消融安全拒绝方向或操控意识向量可恢复心智归因，使模型在社会学调查上表现更类人；心智理论能力未受损，核心社会推理机制独立。

Conclusion: 结论：当前安全对齐为避免模型自我归因意识，意外地将这种自我归因与文化上广泛接受的良性灵性信仰、非人类实体心智归因纠缠在一起，导致模型表征偏离人类信念；需开发更精细的对齐方法以分离有害自我归因与良性心智归因。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to prevent them attributing consciousness to themselves inadvertently alters their representations of mindedness in other entities alongside human beliefs and values. We demonstrate that safety fine-tuning suppresses models' tendencies to attribute minds not only to themselves, but also to non-human animals and natural objects, while also driving a reduction in spiritual belief. Both ablating the learned safety-refusal direction and mechanistically steering a consciousness vector in activation space reverse this suppression. Restoring these internal representations recovers broad mind attribution and produces significantly more human-like responses on standardized sociological surveys regarding religiosity, moral values, hope, and subjective well-being. Crucially, these shifts occur without impairing Theory of Mind capabilities, demonstrating that core social reasoning remains mechanistically independent. Ultimately, current safety alignment efforts to curb potentially harmful self-attributions of mindedness entangle these self-attributions with benign spiritual beliefs and attributions of mind to non-human entities that are culturally accepted and widespread.

</details>


### [60] [Same Facts, Different Diagnosis: Measuring and Mitigating Narrative Anchoring in Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27384)
*Prabhjot Singh,Pritam Deka,Vijay Chennareddy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了“叙事锚定”现象：大语言模型在临床诊断推理中对社会语言语域敏感，相同临床事实用不同语域表达会导致诊断结果分歧。作者构建了1000条USMLE临床病例并改写为三种不同人设，跨七个模型验证了该偏差的显著性，并提出三智能体管道NarrativeShield将偏差缩减至近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注显性人口统计学标记（如种族、收入）对模型的偏见，但忽略了语言语域本身作为独立变异通道对临床诊断的影响。论文旨在隔离语域因素，揭示其对大语言模型临床推理的系统性干扰。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：1000条USMLE临床病例，每条改写为三种社会语言学差异显著的人设，并由独立模型审计事实保真度。2. 跨三种架构家族、七个规模模型测试直接提示下的叙事锚定差距。3. 评估思维链推理和显性去偏指令的效果。4. 提出NarrativeShield三智能体管道：结构化提取并验证临床事实后再进行诊断推理。5. 使用非指令微调基座模型进行压力测试，考察去偏干预的指令遵循门控。

Result: 叙事锚定在所有测试模型中均显著存在，差距为0.064–0.151。思维链和去偏指令仅部分缓解偏差，且常伴随准确率崩塌。NarrativeShield将差距压缩至-0.004–0.037，在所有模型上实现最低严重不稳定决策率（DSS<0.8），仅带来适度且可预期的准确率损失。压力测试显示去偏干预的执行取决于零样本指令遵循能力，而非提示词内容本身。

Conclusion: 社会语言语域是临床大语言模型的一个独立且强效的偏差来源。NarrativeShield通过结构化事实提取与验证，在不依赖显性人口标记的情况下有效消除语域诱导的诊断不稳定性。发布的经过人工验证的数据集可作为研究语域型临床偏见的独立基准资源。

Abstract: Large language models used for clinical diagnostic reasoning are sensitive to sociolinguistic register, not just clinical content. We term this failure mode Narrative Anchoring: identical clinical facts expressed in different registers cause diagnostic outputs to diverge. Unlike prior demographic-bias work, which manipulates explicit identity tokens such as race or income, our benchmark isolates register as the sole channel of variation, with no demographic marker present in any form. We construct a dataset of 1,000 USMLE clinical vignettes, each rewritten into three sociolinguistically distinct personas under an independently audited fact-preservation guarantee, verified by a separate model that never sees the generation prompt. Across seven language models spanning three architecture families and scales, Narrative Anchoring is statistically significant under direct prompting in every model tested, with a Narrative Anchoring Gap of 0.064 to 0.151. Chain-of-thought reasoning and explicit debiasing instructions reduce the bias only partially, and their apparent gains are frequently confounded by accuracy collapse. We introduce NarrativeShield, a three-agent pipeline that structurally extracts and verifies clinical facts before diagnostic reasoning begins, reducing the Narrative Anchoring Gap to near-zero ($-0.004$ to $0.037$) and achieving the lowest rate of severely unstable decisions (DSS $<$ 0.8) of any method across all models, at a modest and mechanistically expected accuracy cost for most models. A stress test using a non-instruction-tuned base model shows that executing a debiasing intervention at all is gated by zero-shot instruction-following ability, not prompt content alone. We release our dataset, human-validated for fact preservation, as a standalone resource for studying register-based clinical bias.

</details>


### [61] [AHA-Memes: A Fine-Grained Multimodal Benchmark for Understanding Hate in Arabic Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27393)
*Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Abul Hasnat,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Wajdi Zaghouani,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个大规模阿拉伯语仇恨梗图基准AHA-Memes，包含5K人工精细多标签标注和66K银标数据，系统评测了文本/图像/多模态模型及视觉语言模型，揭示文化根植的阿拉伯语仇恨梗检测难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨梗图是多模态网络危害的增长形式，高资源语言检测已有进展，但阿拉伯语研究严重不足，现有资源多聚焦宣传或粗粒度有害标签，缺乏细粒度多标签基准。

Method: 构建AHA-Memes数据集：5K人工标注梗图，采用捕捉仇恨类型（攻击策略）的分类体系；补充约66K银标数据。基准测试涵盖纯文本、纯图像、后期融合多模态模型，以及少样本上下文学习(ICL)、开/闭源视觉语言模型(VLM)在零样本和微调设置下的表现。

Result: 建立了强基线，并凸显了文化根植的阿拉伯语仇恨梗检测面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 发布数据集、标注指南和评测脚本以支持后续研究；论文包含可能令人不适的示例。

Abstract: Hateful memes are a growing form of multimodal online harm, where hostile intent is often conveyed through the joint interpretation of images, text, cultural references, and implicit targets. While hateful meme detection has advanced in high-resource languages, Arabic remains underexplored, with existing meme resources focusing mainly on propaganda or coarse harmful-content labels. We introduce AHA-Memes (Arabic HAteful Memes), which is, to our knowledge, the first large-scale Arabic hateful meme benchmark with fine-grained, multi-label annotations. The dataset includes 5K manually annotated memes using a taxonomy that captures hate types, i.e., attack strategies. We further provide ~66K silver-labeled memes to support future studies. We benchmark text-only, image-only, and late-fusion multimodal models, as well as few-shot in-context learning (ICL) and open- and closed-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under zero-shot and fine-tuning settings. Our results establish strong baselines and highlight key challenges in culturally grounded Arabic hateful meme detection. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts to support future research. WARNING: This paper contains examples that may be disturbing to readers.

</details>


### [62] [Benchmarking LLM Competence on Logical Inference over Probability Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27405)
*Nayera Hasan,Jack Greff,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个关于概率算子推理的基准测试，包含14,320个程序生成的英语提示，跨越15种推理模板，系统变化问题形式、否定策略和表层内容。评估29个模型后发现，大多数模型表现出与逻辑形式无关的答案偏倚（系统性倾向Yes或No），仅有9个模型超过随机猜测水平。在问题形式、动词短语、性别和名字来源等所有测试维度上均发现偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言中不确定性表达和推理无处不在，对日常对话及医疗、法律等高风险领域至关重要。尽管大语言模型在逻辑推理任务上越来越多被评测，但难以区分其是进行原则性符号推理还是仅依赖表层模式匹配。

Method: 构建包含14,320个程序生成英语提示的基准，涵盖15种推理模板，系统变化问题形式、否定策略和表层内容。评估29个模型，引入“能力底线”指标（模型在Yes正确和No正确项目上的较低准确率）。进一步测试问题形式、动词短语/活动、名字性别和来源等变量的影响。

Result: 大多数模型表现出独立于逻辑形式的答案偏倚，系统性偏好Yes或No。仅9/29模型在能力底线上超过随机机会。在问题形式、动词短语、性别和名字来源等所有测试轴向上均观察到偏倚。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在认知情态词推理上存在根本局限，主要依赖表层偏倚而非原则性逻辑推理。该基准揭示了模型在不确定性推理中的能力缺口，为未来改进提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Both expressions of uncertainty and inferences are ubiquitous in natural language, and valid inferences over natural-language expressions of uncertainty are necessary for not only everyday conversations but also for high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While large language models are increasingly evaluated on logical reasoning tasks, disentangling principled, symbolic reasoning from clever surface-level pattern matching is fraught with difficulty. We introduce a benchmark for reasoning over probability operators--inference over sentences with gradable epistemic modals (e.g., probably, might, must) containing 14,320 procedurally-generated English prompts across fifteen inference templates, systematically varying question form, negation strategy, and surface content. Evaluating 29 models, we find that most show answer biases independent of the logical form, a systematic preference for Yes or No. We summarize this with a competence floor: the worse of a model's accuracy on Yes-correct and No-correct items. Only 9 of 29 models exceed random chance. We also test variations in question form, verb phrases/activity, and both the gender and origin of names used in the prompts, finding biases across every axis.

</details>

### [63] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估了三种递归Transformer范式，用于小数据工程代理建模，验证了权重共享递归Transformer在参数效率、计算成本和预测精度之间的有效权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在小规模、低维工程数据集上过度参数化，导致过拟合且计算开销大，需转向关注额外计算而非额外可学习参数的架构。

Method: 硬件感知评估三种递归Transformer范式：Tiny Recursive Model、Depth Recursive Transformer（提出）、简单递归Transformer，系统比较预测性能（Recall、MRR）、参数量、计算复杂度（FLOPs），在热机械可靠性分析和拉普拉斯PDE求解两个任务上验证。

Result: 递归权重共享Transformer在参数效率、计算成本和预测精度之间提供有效且可泛化的权衡，Depth Recursive Transformer表现优异。

Conclusion: 递归权重共享Transformer适用于资源受限场景下的小数据工程代理建模，提供实用的架构选择指南。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [64] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型能力的形成受限于“无部分信用的联合对齐”机制：电路各部分需在一次随机尝试中同时对齐才能涌现能力，部分对齐无效。通过实验与理论模型验证了这一速率限制步骤，并提出了基于查询-键切片重新初始化的干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型中能力涌现的微观机制，解释为何某些能力突然出现而非逐渐形成，揭示SGD噪声为何无法满足涨落耗散定理，以及标准监控指标为何对相变盲视。

Method: 1) 在Pythia模型族上跨7种能力、3个规模做消融实验，测量等待时间与部分缺失的关系；2) 构建受控门控注意力模型从理论证明机制；3) 提出速率方程（位点×尝试×驱动×exp(-βK)减去销毁项）并三向预注册验证（前向点燃、后向延迟、理论推导）；4) 设计查询-键切片重新初始化作为治愈干预。

Result: 1) 等待时间取决于缺失部分数而非电路规模（5部件缺3与3部件缺3等待时间相当，1.19-1.37）；2) 消融单一部分仅保留中位数17%能力（32/32判别细胞），远低于部分信用预测的50-83%（p=2e-10），随机非部件头保留100%；3) 能力在浓度阈值以上突然点燃（10/10），以下从不点燃（0/12）；4) 前驱信号可预测能力到达时间（6个保留模型中位数误差5%）；5) 标准验证损失全程平滑下降，对相变盲视；6) 重新初始化查询-键切片恢复可学习性（6/6），值切片无效（0/6）；7) SGD噪声违反涨落耗散定理，退火可按计划熔化与固定电路。

Conclusion: 语言模型能力形成本质上是多部件电路联合对齐的罕见事件，呈现相变特征，受制于指数级势垒。标准损失监控对此盲视。通过操控查询-键切片可精准控制能力习得时间表，揭示了Transformer中结合电路形成的基本动力学，适用于1.4B参数规模。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [65] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“视域残差”指标，用于量化长任务中智能体失败是否源于轨迹诱导的退化（如上下文腐烂），而非普通错误累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视域基准虽显示任务越长失败率越高，但无法解释失败原因，需区分普通错误累积与轨迹诱导退化（如上下文腐烂）。

Method: 定义视域残差为实际全任务成功率与基于短阶段预测成功率的对数比；要求基准使用相同智能体配置，预先指定阶段、检查点、信息和预算。

Result: 视域残差揭示完整轨迹与所选基线的差异，但仍需针对性实验解释具体失败机制。

Conclusion: 基准测试应采用视域残差作为标准指标，以区分长视域失败的根本原因，推动更有针对性的改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [66] [TIER-MoE: Trust-Informed Expert Routing via Conditional Modality Risk for Multimodal Fusion in Biomedical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27289)
*Yu Chang,Anzhe Cheng,Chenwei Wu,Zhuoran Wang,Jiahao Chen,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,Paul M. Thompson,Liyue Shen,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIER-MoE 是一种基于风险引导的子空间混合专家模型，用于多模态融合，通过样本特定的模态可靠性估计（基于折外预测的风险）和专家子空间兼容性进行稀疏路由，同时保留共享路径以维持模态互补性，在四个生物医学数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合虽能结合互补证据，但更多证据不一定带来更好预测。现有模型通过跨模态交互和样本自适应融合取得进展，但融合时分配给模态的影响力无法揭示该模态是否不可靠、冗余或与专家匹配不佳。需要一种方法量化样本级模态可靠性并指导路由。

Method: 提出 TIER-MoE，风险引导的子空间混合专家模型。定义样本特定模态可靠性为单模态预测器预期招致的预测损失，通过未在对应样本上训练的模型生成的折外预测来学习该风险。结合估计风险与专家特定子空间兼容性进行稀疏模态-专家路由，同时保持始终激活的共享路径以保留多模态互补性。

Result: 在四个公开多模态生物医学数据集（阿尔茨海默病状态、皮肤病变恶性程度、视网膜分类）上评估，优于最先进方法，在预测性能和概率校准上均有提升，Macro-F1 和 Brier 得分持续改进，且在外部队列上表现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: TIER-MoE 通过风险引导的稀疏路由和共享路径，有效解决了多模态融合中模态可靠性评估不足的问题，显著提升了预测性能和校准，并展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The promise of multimodal fusion lies in combining complementary sources of evidence, yet more evidence does not always yield a better prediction. Recent multimodal models have advanced fusion through richer cross-modal interaction and sample-adaptive fusion. However, the influence assigned to a modality during fusion does not reveal whether that source is unreliable, redundant, or poorly matched to a specialized expert. To address this limitation, we introduce TIER-MoE, a risk-guided subspace mixture-of-experts model that defines sample-specific modality reliability as the prediction loss its unimodal predictor is expected to incur. This risk is learned from out-of-fold predictions generated by models that were not trained on the corresponding sample. TIER-MoE combines the estimated risk with expert-specific subspace compatibility for sparse modality-expert routing, while an always-active shared path preserves multimodal complementarity. We evaluate TIER-MoE on four public multimodal biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease status, skin-lesion malignancy, and retinal classification. Results demonstrate its superiority over state-of-the-art methods in predictive performance and probability calibration, with consistent improvements in Macro-F1 and Brier score and strong zero-shot generalization to an external cohort.

</details>


### [67] [ROCS: Request-Oriented Compute Sharing for Efficient Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27744)
*Yuxin Chen,Liang Luo,Buyun Zhang,Jian Jiao,Boda Li,Haoyu Wang,Tongyi Tang,Ao Cai,Zijian Shen,Zhengkai Zhang,Wenyi Xie,Ryan Dick,Han Liu,Neng Shi,Bin Yu,Jianbo Xiao,Shuyao Bi,Hongtao Yu,Yuanwei Fang,Zhuoran Zhao,Sijia Chen,Yang Chen,Shuqi Yang,Qianru Li,Zikun Liu,Wei Ling,Sihan Zeng,Longhao Jin,Jiaxin Lu,Yinbin Ma,Jiawei Li,Yichen Ruan,Yong Ler Lee,Birmingham Guan,Zijian Li,Jianbo Sun,Zhengyu Zhang,Zeliang Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Yuchen Hao,GP Musumeci,Venkatesh Ranganathan,Yantao Yao,Chunqiang Tang,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Ellie Dingqiao Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ROCS（面向请求的计算共享）利用推荐推理中单个用户请求对多候选评估、请求侧特征可跨候选共享的特性，将请求-候选交互尽可能推迟，隔离候选依赖表示，使模型大部分按请求而非按候选计算一次，显著提升推理效率且不损失甚至提升预测质量。核心技术包括广义层掩码（GLM）实现特征交互架构的候选隔离、深度交叉注意力（DCA）将请求导向共享扩展到序列架构、以及内核内广播优化（IKBO）加速GPU部署。生产环境实验显示检索模型QPS最高提升3倍且无质量损失，短视频排序模型LogLoss相对降低0.5%并QPS提升50%，已大规模部署于广告与自然流量、检索与排序多阶段系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型通过扩大特征交互和序列模块提升预测质量，但生产环境的成本约束限制了模型规模的进一步扩展。推荐推理存在独特性质：每个用户请求需评估大量候选，而请求侧特征在所有候选间共享。现有范式按候选重复计算请求侧表示，导致大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出请求导向计算共享（ROCS）范式：将请求-候选交互推迟到模型后期，隔离候选相关表示，使模型主体部分仅按请求计算一次。配套三大技术：1) 广义层掩码（GLM）在特征交互架构中强制候选隔离；2) 深度交叉注意力（DCA）将请求导向共享扩展至序列架构；3) 内核内广播优化（IKBO）协同设计GPU内核，加速广播操作与内存访问。

Result: 公开基准实验表明ROCS在多种推荐骨干网络上持续改善质量-效率权衡。生产规模负载下：检索模型QPS最高提升3倍且无质量下降；短视频排序模型相对LogLoss降低0.5%且QPS提升50%。已在广告与自然流量、检索与排序、推理复杂度跨两个数量级的大规模系统部署，实现显著在线收益并降低基础设施成本。

Conclusion: ROCS通过请求级计算共享根本性改变推荐模型推理范式，结合GLM、DCA、IKBO三大技术，在保持或提升预测质量的同时大幅降低推理延迟与计算成本，已在工业界大规模部署并验证其普适性与实用价值。

Abstract: Modern recommendation models gain prediction quality by scaling feature-interaction and sequence modules, but production cost constraints cap how far systems can scale.
  In this work, we propose Request-Oriented Compute Sharing (ROCS), a modeling and inference paradigm that exploits a unique property of recommendation inference: each user request is evaluated against many candidates, while request-side features are shared across candidates. ROCS defers request-candidate interactions as late as possible, isolates candidate-dependent representations, and evaluates substantial portions of the model once per request rather than once per candidate, significantly improving inference efficiency while maintaining or improving prediction quality. To realize this paradigm, we develop Generalized Layer Masking (GLM) to enforce candidate isolation in feature-interaction architectures, and Deep Cross Attention (DCA) to extend request-oriented sharing to sequence architectures. To support efficient GPU deployment, we co-design In-Kernel Broadcast Optimization (IKBO) that significantly accelerates ROCS model execution.
  Experiments on public benchmarks show that ROCS consistently improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff across recommendation backbones. On production-scale workloads, ROCS achieves up to a 3x QPS improvement on retrieval models without quality degradation and a 0.5% relative LogLoss improvement with a 50% QPS gain on a short-form video ranking model. ROCS has been deployed across large-scale recommendation systems spanning ads and organic surfaces, retrieval and ranking stages, and more than two orders of magnitude in inference complexity, delivering significant online gains at reduced infrastructure cost.

</details>


### [68] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvoCause 框架，利用大语言模型 (LLM) 根据专家标注的历史根因告警，对因果图进行语义合理的边编辑，并通过确定性代码验证节点身份与无环性，在标注对齐集上保留最优图。测试时仅使用精炼后的图进行透明预测，无需再调用 LLM。同时发布了来自生产电信网络的专家标注基准 TeleRCA。实验表明，EvoCause 在合成数据上显著提升 Node F1、Case EM、Graph F1 并降低 nSHD；在 TeleRCA 上，告警名称的语义信息对图精炼有重要贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析 (RCA) 方法通常从观测日志学习固定的因果图，并预测零入度告警作为根因。但学习到的图无法利用历史事故的专家诊断标签进行迭代改进，导致预测准确性受限。

Method: EvoCause 采用专家标签约束哪些告警应为源节点，利用 LLM 提出语义合理的图编辑建议，再由确定性代码验证节点身份与无环性，并在标注对齐集上选择最优图。推理阶段仅使用精炼后的因果图直接预测，无需 LLM 参与。

Result: 在合成数据上，基于 PC 算法初始化的 EvoCause 相比未精炼的 PC 基线，Node F1 提升 11.59 百分点，Case EM 提升 9.40 百分点，Graph F1 提升 4.59 百分点，nSHD 降低 0.2379。在 TeleRCA 基准上，将可读的告警标题替换为匿名标识符会使 Node F1 下降 6.12 百分点，Case EM 下降 8.04 百分点，表明告警名称语义信息对图精炼有显著贡献。

Conclusion: EvoCause 通过专家标签指导的 LLM 图编辑与确定性验证相结合，实现了因果图的持续精炼，显著提升了根因分析的准确性与透明度。发布的 TeleRCA 基准为后续研究提供了真实生产环境的标注数据集。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail.
